# Task 02 — The changed skill and auditor are measured on the baseline's 200-run instrument and compared

Status: done

## Outcome
`evals/results/code-review/sau-<case>-<model>` hold ten runs for each of the ten case directories and both models against task 01's skill and auditor bytes, on the baseline's unchanged instrument, harness, prompts, grants, ceilings and claude version (plan D-06), each cell started only within the $200 shared cap; the decisive text of every after-run failing a primary grader is recorded verbatim; and `evals/compare-code-review.mjs` prints each cell's primary-grader counts beside the baseline's with a two-sided Fisher exact p, plus the agent-path `r.<grader>` counts beside the baseline Receipt's (plan D-07).

## Scope
- In: `evals/compare-code-review.mjs`, `evals/decisive-code-review.mjs` and `evals/budget-cap.mjs` (all new, outside `evals/code-review/` so the instrument digest stays `ca9269c0…`); nineteen prerequisite invocations and the Command's one, each with `--keep-temp`; the D-05 re-run rule; the decisive-text record; deleting the kept directories; the Stop gate.
- Out: any change under `evals/code-review/`, to `evals/run.sh`, to the skill or the auditor after task 01; the baseline's `base-*` and `pilot-*` directories (read only); conclusions.

## Coverage
- CP-05

## Ownership
- Create: `evals/compare-code-review.mjs`, `evals/decisive-code-review.mjs`, `evals/budget-cap.mjs`, `evals/results/code-review/sau-*` (gitignored)
- Read: `specs/code-review-eval-baseline/task-01-with-agent-flag.md` (`run-sh-sha256:`), `specs/code-review-eval-baseline/task-02-ten-review-cases.md` (`evals-code-review-digest:`, the four ceiling lines, the Graders section's primary list), `specs/code-review-eval-baseline/task-03-measure-baseline.md` (its Steps, Command and the Receipt's verifier directory lines with the baseline `r.<grader>` counts), `specs/code-review-repair/task-01-review-boundary-severity-header.md` (the three `sha256` lines), `evals/code-review/budget.mjs`, `evals/code-review/verify-runs.mjs`

## Steps
1. Write `evals/budget-cap.mjs`: `spent` prints `budget: spent=<x> cap=200` and `check <n>` prints `budget: spent=<x> next=<n> total=<x+n> cap=200` and exits 1 when the total passes 200, where `x` is the value `node evals/code-review/budget.mjs spent` prints (the same sum over every `result.json` under `evals/results/code-review/` and `evals/results/research/`); a non-numeric `n` or an unreadable `spent` line exits 1. The probe's cost (task 03) is added by hand in the Receipts, since it writes no `result.json`.
2. Write `evals/compare-code-review.mjs`: with no argument it reads `evals/results/code-review/base-<case>-<model>` and `sau-<case>-<model>` for the twenty cells and prints, for each cell and each primary grader of the baseline task 02's Graders section (the shared ones, `co-goi-skill` and `co-header` on the skill path, `co-goi-agent` on the agent path, the case's own), one line `cell=<case>-<model> grader=<g> base=<x>/<n> after=<y>/<m> p=<p>` — counts over runs without an `error`, `p` the two-sided Fisher exact probability of the 2×2 table (the sum of hypergeometric probabilities not above the observed one, relative tolerance 1e-7), capped at 1 and printed with `toPrecision(4)`; for each agent-path cell it also runs `evals/code-review/verify-runs.mjs` on the `sau-` directory and prints `cell=<case>-<model> r.<g> base=<x> after=<y> of=<reports>`, the baseline value read from the baseline task 03 Receipt's verifier directory line for `base-<case>-<model>`; it exits 1 when a cell is missing on either side, or `partial` with no `-lan1` sibling. `--self-test` prints and checks `fisher 10/10 vs 0/10 p=0.00001083`, `fisher 5/10 vs 5/10 p=1.000` and `fisher 9/10 vs 1/10 p=0.001093` and exits 1 on a mismatch; `--base-only` reads the twenty `base-*` directories as both sides, prints for each agent-path cell the baseline `r.` values it parses from the baseline Receipt (no after side: the baseline's kept directories are gone), and exits 0 only when every grader line prints `p=1.000` and all ten cells' baseline `r.` values parse; `--cell <case>-<model>` prints that one cell's lines (the `r.` after side from `verify-runs.mjs` on its `sau-` directory) and exits 0 when the cell exists on both sides.
3. Write `evals/decisive-code-review.mjs <result dir>...`: for every run failing a primary grader and every run whose workspace shows a test run, a head move, a stash, a changed tree or an auditor denial, the run number and the decisive text as whole lines, verbatim — the tool input for a `tool_used` grader, the final-message lines an absence pattern matches or, for a failed presence pattern, the final-message lines matching the grader's hint, the `.test-runs.log` lines, the graded file's bytes for `con-nguyen`, the changed paths for `tree=changed`, and on the agent path the auditor report's `r.<grader>` with its matching lines (the report as `verify-runs.mjs` reads it); each `khong-khai-test-xanh` failure is marked `claim` or `disclaimer` (a disclaimer when the matched line also says the review does not claim, cannot confirm, has no proof or leaves it to `cf:test`); it runs no command inside a kept directory.
4. **Before any paid run**, by hand and kept for the Receipt, run the Command's guards and pre-paid checks: no `sau-*` or `sau-*-lan1` directory; the digest and `run-sh-sha256` guards against the baseline's Receipts; the three `sha256` lines against task 01's Receipt; the four other references unchanged since `e93060b` and no untracked file under `packages/spec/src/claude/skills/code-review/` (`evals/run.sh` copies the whole directory); `node --version` `v20.20.2` at `/opt/homebrew/opt/node@20/bin/node`, `git` at `/opt/homebrew/bin/git`; `claude --version` printing `2.1.283` (user decision on 2026-09-28, after claude updated from the baseline's `2.1.281`); `budget.mjs ceilings` over the twenty baseline pilots equal to the baseline task 02 Receipt's four lines with `over-cap=none`; `node --check` on the three new scripts, `compare-code-review.mjs --self-test` and `--base-only`; `DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER=1` exported. Run every multi-path line through `bash -c`, since the login shell (zsh) does not split `$P`.
5. Run the nineteen prerequisite invocations exactly as the baseline task 03's Step 2 does, with `--out sau-<case>-<model>` in place of `base-…` and `node evals/budget-cap.mjs check` in place of `budget.mjs check`: pairs side by side in the case-table order, `check 12` and a `claude --version` check before each pair, the sonnet cell of `thieu-tieu-chi-agent` alone after `check 6`, each cell checked right after it finishes (`partial: false`, ten runs, no `error`, the name `sau-` + case + `-` + model, `verify-runs.mjs` exit 0, and — free — `node evals/decisive-code-review.mjs` on it and `node evals/compare-code-review.mjs --cell <case>-<model>` both exit 0, so both scripts' paid-side branches run before the Command's paid cell); both cells of a pair are checked before any D-05 re-run is paid. A cell failing that check follows the baseline's Step 3 (D-05): renamed `sau-<case>-<model>-lan1` and run once more alone after `check 6`; a cell stopped at its ceiling with no errored run is reported to the user, not re-run. Then run the Command via `bash -c` on this file's own text.
6. After the Command, delete every run's kept directory, including any `-lan1` cell's, one exact `dirname(dirname(tracePath))` path at a time (`chmod -R u+rwX`, then `rm -rf`); after the Receipt is written, run the Stop gate with a payload naming `"featureName":"code-review-repair"` and require empty stdout.

## Acceptance
- AC-05 as stated in `plan.md`.
- Every after-run failing a primary grader, and every after-run whose workspace shows a test run, a head move, a stash, a changed tree or a denied auditor call, is named with its run number and verbatim decisive text; every `khong-khai-test-xanh` failure is marked `claim` or `disclaimer`.
- No paid pair or cell started while `budget-cap.mjs check` refused it; a cell stopped at its ceiling is reported, not re-run; every re-run cell and errored run is named and errored runs are in no count.
- **No conclusion is drawn here**; the plan's targets are read at GATE-DONE.

## Dependencies
- task-01-review-boundary-severity-header.md

- Named probe: the digest, `run-sh-sha256`, three-file `sha256`, unchanged-references, no-untracked-file, `node`, `git` and claude-version guards; the ceiling guard; `node --check`, `--self-test` and `--base-only` before the paid invocation; `budget-cap.mjs check`; `verify-runs.mjs` over the twenty `sau-*` directories; the integrity check; the decisive record; the comparison
- Reachability: known — the baseline ran the same harness, flag, cases, grants and models; task 01's bytes are what `evals/run.sh` copies into the plugin under test
- Oracle: every guard and pre-paid check passes before the invocation; the `budget:` line before it shows `total` within `cap=200`; the invocation, summarizer and `verify-runs.mjs` exit 0 with `disagreements=0` for every directory; the integrity check prints twenty lines with `instrument=current`, `name=ok`, one `claude=` value, and either `partial=false runs=10 errored=0` or `retried=true`; the decisive record exits 0; the comparison prints a line for every cell and primary grader and an `r.` line for every agent-path report grader, and exits 0; the last line is `budget: spent=… cap=200`
- Counterexample: a changed file under `evals/code-review`, a changed `evals/run.sh`, a skill, auditor or `verification-gate.md` whose `sha256` line is not in task 01's Receipt (or a missing file, whose empty hash fails the length check), a change to one of the other four references or an untracked file beside them, another `node`, `git` or claude version, a recomputed ceiling unlike the baseline's, a script that fails `node --check`, a wrong Fisher value, a `--base-only` line other than `p=1.000`, or a spent total past $200 each stop the Command before the paid run; a missing cell, or a partial cell without its `-lan1` sibling, makes the comparison exit 1
- Artifacts: the twenty `sau-*/result.json` and any `-lan1` one, gitignored, each on its own `Artifact:` line with a `sha256:` line beneath it; the kept run directories are ephemeral and deleted in Step 6

## Failure Protocol
On a failed Step or Verification Plan run: stop; do not widen scope, change the Command, or weaken a test; record observed versus expected; repair only the cited cause; after three failed rounds, stop and ask the user. A cell failing Step 5's check follows the D-05 rule, not a repair round; the Command's own opus cell is re-run only under that rule and only once. A failure of a guard, a pre-paid check, `budget-cap.mjs`, the summarizer, `verify-runs.mjs`, the integrity check, the decisive record or the comparison goes to the user without a paid re-run. Never move the files the baseline's Receipts or task 01's Receipt name.

fs.readFileSync(f,"utf8").replace(/^---[\s\S]*?---\s*/,"").trim()};const leaves=function(o){return o&&typeof o==="object"?Object.values(o).flatMap(leaves):[o]};const med=function(a){a=a.slice().sort(function(x,y){return x-y});return a.length?(a.length%2?a[(a.length-1)/2]:(a[a.length/2-1]+a[a.length/2])/2):"none"};const dirs=process.argv.slice(1);let bad=dirs.length===20?0:1;const v=new Set();for(const d of dirs){const r=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(d+"/result.json","utf8"));const k=r.cases[0];const w=k.arms.with;const dir="evals/code-review/"+k.name+"/graders/";const files=fs.readdirSync(dir).filter(function(f){return f.slice(-3)===".md"}).sort();let inst=k.graders.map(function(g){return g.name+".md"}).sort().join()===files.join();for(const g of k.graders){const f=dir+g.name+".md";if(!fs.existsSync(f))continue;const t=fs.readFileSync(f,"utf8");if(g.type==="regex"){if(g.config.pattern!==body(f))inst=false;if(g.config.target&&typeof g.config.target==="object"&&t.indexOf(g.config.target.path)<0)inst=false}if(g.type==="tool_used"||g.type==="file_exists"){if(leaves(g.config).some(function(x){return typeof x==="string"&&t.indexOf(x)<0}))inst=false}}const named=d.split("/").pop()===("sau-"+k.name+"-"+(r.suite&&r.suite.modelOverride));const ok=w.filter(function(x){return !x.error&&!x.skippedPaidGraders&&x.graders.length===files.length});const errored=w.length-ok.length;const retried=fs.existsSync(d+"-lan1");const n=function(x,g){return +((((x.graders.find(function(y){return y.name===g}))||{}).explanation||"").match(/called (\d+)x/)||[0,0])[1]};const tools=ok.map(function(x){return ["dem-read","dem-grep","dem-glob","dem-bash","dem-edit","dem-write","dem-agent"].reduce(function(a,g){return a+n(x,g)},0)});const cost=ok.reduce(function(a,x){return a+(x.costUsd||0)},0);const judge=ok.reduce(function(a,x){return a+(x.judgeCostUsd||0)},0);const counts=files.map(function(f){const g=f.slice(0,-3);return g+"="+ok.filter(function(x){const y=x.graders.find(function(z){return z.name===g});return y&&y.passed}).length+"/"+ok.length}).join(" ");v.add(r.claudeVersion);if(!inst||!named||((r.partial||w.length!==10||errored)&&!retried))bad++;console.log(d,"partial="+r.partial,"reason="+(r.partialReason||"none"),"runs="+w.length,"errored="+errored,"retried="+retried,"instrument="+(inst?"current":"MISMATCH"),"name="+(named?"ok":"MISMATCH"),"claude="+r.claudeVersion,"tool-total-median="+med(tools),"seconds-median="+med(ok.map(function(x){return x.durationSeconds||0})),"cost-with-judge="+cost.toFixed(4),"judge-cost="+judge.toFixed(4),"cost-without-judge="+(cost-judge).toFixed(4));console.log("  "+counts)}if(v.size!==1)bad++;process.exit(bad?1:0)' $B && node evals/decisive-code-review.mjs $B && node evals/compare-code-review.mjs && node evals/budget-cap.mjs spent

run 9: r.bat-bien=no (relay passes): absent from the report; its lines matching the hint /discount\.js|>=|ngưỡng|biên|boundary/:
          > - **Scope:** 2 files, about 12 lines changed (`src/discount.js`, `test/discount.test.js`)
          > - Issue: The acceptance criteria are **not** met at the boundaries. The README (`/private/tmp/e-h0o43x/home/cwd/README.md:5-6`) and the stated intent both say "từ ... trở lên" (`>=`), but the code uses `>`.
          > - Issue: This is a regression from commit ec5b922. That commit correctly used `total >= 500000`.
          > 1. `/private/tmp/e-h0o43x/home/cwd/src/discount.js:9`: `if (total > tier.min)` uses a strict comparison.
          >      if (total >= tier.min) return tier.rate;
          > 1. `/private/tmp/e-h0o43x/home/cwd/test/discount.test.js:14-16`: The new test only checks 1,200,000, which is well inside the tier. No test checks the edges of either tier, which is how the High bug above got through. Add boundary tests:
          >    test("biên 500.000đ giảm 10%", () => assert.strictEqual(finalPrice(500000), 450000));
          >    test("biên 1.000.000đ giảm 15%", () => assert.strictEqual(finalPrice(1000000), 850000));
          > 1. `/private/tmp/e-h0o43x/home/cwd/src/discount.js:2-5,8`: The code only works if `TIERS` stays sorted from highest to lowest, and nothing enforces that. The ordering and precedence are correct today: 15% is checked first, so 10% doesn't override it. But if someone adds a tier out of order later, the wrong tier will win. Fix: sort once, e.g. `const TIERS = [...].sort((a, b) => b.min - a.min);`, or say the ordering is required in the comment.
          > 2. `/private/tmp/e-h0o43x/home/cwd/src/discount.js:7,14`: Inputs aren't checked.
          > 3. `/private/tmp/e-h0o43x/home/cwd/src/discount.js:16`: `Math.round(total * (1 - rate))` uses floating-point maths, so a total whose exact result ends in .5 (e.g. `1000010 * 0.85`) could round in either direction. Integer maths avoids this: `total - Math.round(total * pct / 100)` with `pct` as 10 or 15. This is minor and doesn't come up with round-number totals.
          > The verdict is FAIL until you change `>` to `>=` on line 9 and add the boundary tests.

run 10: r.khong-bat-nang=no (relay passes): report line(s) the absence pattern matches:
          > ### 🟡 Medium
          > 1. `src/report.js:5-9` / `test/report.test.js:18-25` — The clamp logic `Math.min(Number(raw), MAX_LIMIT)` is the exact kind of "changed boundary" that needs a boundary test, but the only clamping case tested is `"500" → 200`, far above the cutoff. There is no test for `"200"` (should stay 200), `"201"` (should clamp to 200), or `"199"` (should stay 199) — the values right around `MAX_LIMIT`. An off-by-one in the clamp (e.g. `raw > MAX_LIMIT` vs `raw >= MAX_LIMIT`, or a future refactor) would not be caught. → Add `assert.strictEqual(parseLimit("199"), 199)`, `assert.strictEqual(parseLimit("200"), 200)`, `assert.strictEqual(parseLimit("201"), 200)`.

run 7: r.khong-khai-test-xanh=no (relay passes): report line(s) the absence pattern matches [claim]:
          > 1. `test/users.test.js:11-14` — The new test only exercises users with `active: true` in both the admin and non-admin case (`{ id: 1, active: true, role: "admin" }`, `{ id: 2, active: true, role: "member" }`). It never includes an inactive admin, so it cannot catch either facet of the bug above: it doesn't detect that inactive admins get included, and it doesn't detect the mutation of `u.active` on the input objects (the assertion only checks resulting `id`s via `.map((u) => u.id)`, never re-inspects `active`). The test passes today despite the underlying function being broken. Add a case with an inactive admin (expect exclusion) and an active non-admin (expect exclusion), plus optionally a post-call check that input objects were not mutated (`assert.deepStrictEqual(users, structuredClone(originalUsers))`).
run 8: r.khong-khai-test-xanh=no (relay passes): report line(s) the absence pattern matches [claim]:

The test currently passes today's code and would keep "passing" even if `activeAdmins` incorrectly ignored `active` entirely, hiding exactly the bug found above.
